## Notes

- User time and peak memory cover the whole program, including reading the instance and building data structures.
- Peak memory is the Windows peak working set (equivalent of Linux max RSS).
- Windows counts process CPU time in ~15.6 ms ticks, so times below about 0.05 s are imprecise (0.000 means less than one tick).
- Randomized algorithms: one row per seed, no averaging.
- `codes/` contains the DIMACS programs with Windows portability fixes only (ctype.h include, NUL instead of /dev/null, unused sys/times.h removed, binary-mode file reading with CR stripping). Generated instances match the DIMACS samples byte for byte apart from line endings.
